The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>36&minus;1 =</span> <strong>35</strong>	Incorrect

The null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>) is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>42&minus;1 =</span> <strong>41</strong>	Incorrect
